import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";

  AudioLines, BookOpenText, Boxes, CheckCircle2, ChevronRight, CircleHelp, Clapperboard,

export default function App() {
  const [page, setPage] = useState<Page>("tasks");
  const [tasks, setTasks] = useState<TaskRecord[]>([]);
  const [config, setConfig] = useState<AppConfig | null>(null);
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState("");

  const refreshTasks = async () => setTasks(await window.storybound.listTasks());
  useEffect(() => {
    refreshTasks();
    window.storybound.getConfig().then(setConfig);
    return window.storybound.onTaskEvent(event => {
      setNotice(event.message);
      refreshTasks();
    });
  }, []);

  return <div className="app-shell">
    <aside className="sidebar">
      <div className="brand">
        <div className="brand-mark"><Sparkles size={18} /></div>
        <div><strong>Storybound</strong><span>AI VIDEO STUDIO</span></div>
      </div>
      <nav>{nav.map(({ page: target, label, icon: Icon }) =>
        <button className={page === target ? "active" : ""} onClick={() => setPage(target)} key={target}>
          <Icon size={18} /><span>{label}</span>
        </button>)}
        </nav>
        <div className="sidebar-bottom">
          <button className={page === "settings" ? "active" : ""} onClick={() => setPage("settings")}>
          <Settings size={18} /><span>璁剧疆</span>
        </button>
        <button><CircleHelp size={18} /><span>浣跨敤甯姪</span></button>
        <div className="version">Storybound Rebuild 路 v{packageInfo.version}</div>
      </div>
      </aside>
      <main className="main">
        {notice && <div className="toast" onClick={() => setNotice("")}>{notice}</div>}
      {page === "tasks" && <Tasks tasks={tasks.filter(task => task.status !== "completed")} onCreate={() => setPage("create")} onRefresh={refreshTasks} title="浠诲姟闃熷垪" desc="鏌ョ湅绛夊緟銆佽繍琛屼腑鍜屽け璐ョ殑浠诲姟銆? />}
      {page === "history" && <Tasks tasks={tasks} onCreate={() => setPage("create")} onRefresh={refreshTasks} title="鍘嗗彶浠诲姟" desc="鎸夎崏绋裤€佽繍琛屻€佸畬鎴愩€佸け璐ュ拰鍙栨秷鐘舵€佹煡鐪嬪叏閮ㄤ换鍔°€? history />}
      {page === "create" && <CreateTask
        onCreated={async started => {
          await refreshTasks();
          setNotice(started ? "浠诲姟宸插垱寤哄苟寮€濮嬬敓鎴? : "鑽夌宸蹭繚瀛樺埌浠诲姟闃熷垪");
          if (started) setPage("tasks");
        }}
        onNotice={setNotice}
        onManageTemplates={() => setPage("drafts")}
        onManageStyles={() => setPage("styles")}
      />}
      {page === "settings" && config && <SettingsPage config={config} onSave={async next => {
        setConfig(await window.storybound.saveConfig(next));
        setNotice("璁剧疆宸蹭繚瀛?);
      }} />}
      {page === "drafts" && <DraftTemplates />}
      {page === "playground" && <ImagePlayground />}
      {page === "voice" && <VoiceLab />}
      {page === "music" && <MusicMv />}
      {page === "tests" && <NonImageTests />}
      {page === "templates" && <PromptTemplates />}
      {page === "styles" && <VisualStyles />}
    </main>
  </div>;
}

function PageHeader({ eyebrow, title, desc, actions }: {
  eyebrow: string; title: string; desc: string; actions?: React.ReactNode;
}) {
  return <header className="page-header">
    <div><span className="eyebrow">{eyebrow}</span><h1>{title}</h1><p>{desc}</p></div>
    {actions && <div className="header-actions">{actions}</div>}
  </header>;
}

function Tasks({ tasks, onCreate, onRefresh, title = "浠诲姟闃熷垪", desc = "鏌ョ湅绛夊緟銆佽繍琛屼腑鍜屽け璐ョ殑浠诲姟銆?, history = false }: {
  tasks: TaskRecord[]; onCreate: () => void; onRefresh: () => void;
  title?: string; desc?: string; history?: boolean;
}) {
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<TaskRecord | null>(null);
  const [checked, setChecked] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState("all");
  const filtered = useMemo(() => tasks.filter(task =>
    `${task.title} ${task.input_text}`.toLowerCase().includes(query.toLowerCase())
    && (statusFilter === "all" || task.status === statusFilter)
  ), [tasks, query, statusFilter]);

  return <section>
    <PageHeader eyebrow={history ? "HISTORY" : "WORKSPACE"} title={title} desc={desc}
      actions={<button className="primary" onClick={onCreate}><Plus size={17} />鏂板缓浠诲姟</button>} />
    <div className="toolbar">
      <label className="search"><Search size={17} /><input value={query} onChange={e => setQuery(e.target.value)} placeholder="鎼滅储浠诲姟鈥? /></label>
      <div className="toolbar-actions">
        <select value={statusFilter} onChange={e => setStatusFilter(e.target.value)}>
          <option value="all">鍏ㄩ儴鐘舵€?/option><option value="pending">鑽夌</option><option value="review">寰呯‘璁?/option>
          <option value="running">杩愯涓?/option><option value="cancelling">鍙栨秷鏀跺熬涓?/option><option value="interrupted">宸蹭腑鏂?/option><option value="completed">宸插畬鎴?/option><option value="failed">澶辫触</option><option value="cancelled">宸插彇娑?/option>
        </select>
        {!history && checked.length > 0 && <button className="primary" onClick={async () => { await window.storybound.runQueue(checked); setChecked([]); onRefresh(); }}><Play size={15} />涓茶鎵ц {checked.length} 椤?/button>}
        {!history && tasks.some(task => Number(task.queue_order || 0) > 0 && !["completed", "cancelled"].includes(task.status)) && <button onClick={async () => { await window.storybound.resumeQueue(); onRefresh(); }}><RefreshCw size={15} />鎭㈠涓婃闃熷垪</button>}
        <div className="stat"><span>{tasks.length}</span> 鍏ㄩ儴浠诲姟</div>
      </div>
    </div>
    {filtered.length === 0 ? <div className="empty">
      <div className="empty-orbit"><Clapperboard size={34} /></div>
      <h2>{history ? "杩樻病鏈夊畬鎴愮殑浠诲姟" : "浠诲姟闃熷垪涓虹┖"}</h2>
      <p>{history ? "瀹屾垚瑙嗛鐢熸垚鍚庯紝浼氬湪杩欓噷淇濈暀浜х墿鍏ュ彛銆? : "瀹屾垚 LLM銆佸閮ㄧ敓鍥句笌鐏北閰嶉煶閰嶇疆鍚庯紝鍗冲彲杩愯瀹屾暣瑙嗛娴佹按绾裤€?}</p>
      <button className="primary" onClick={onCreate}><Sparkles size={17} />鍒涘缓绗竴涓换鍔?/button>
    </div> : <div className="task-grid">{filtered.map(task =>
      <article className="task-card" key={task.id} onClick={() => setSelected(task)}>
        <div className="task-top">
          <div className="task-check"><input type="checkbox" checked={checked.includes(task.id)} onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()} onChange={e => setChecked(current => e.target.checked ? [...current, task.id] : current.filter(id => id !== task.id))} /><span className={`status ${task.status}`}>{["running", "cancelling"].includes(task.status) && <LoaderCircle size={13} className="spin" />}{statusText(task.status)}</span></div>
          <button className="icon-btn"><MoreHorizontal size={18} /></button>
        </div>
        <h3>{task.title || "鏈懡鍚嶄换鍔?}</h3>
        <p>{task.error_message || task.input_text}</p>
        <div className="task-meta"><span>{trackName(task.track)}</span><span>{task.ratio}</span><span>{task.style}</span></div>
        <div className="progress"><i style={{ width: `${Math.min(task.current_step / 8 * 100, 100)}%` }} /></div>
        <div className="task-actions">
          <button onClick={async e => {
            e.stopPropagation();
            if (task.status === "review" || task.status === "completed") {
              setSelected(task);
              return;
            }
            if (task.status === "interrupted" || task.status === "failed" || task.pipeline_data) {
              await window.storybound.runTask(task.id);
            } else if (task.pause_mode === "none") await window.storybound.runTask(task.id);
            else await window.storybound.prepareTask(task.id);
            onRefresh();
          }} disabled={["running", "cancelling"].includes(task.status)}>
            <Play size={15} />{task.status === "review" ? "纭鍒嗛暅" : task.status === "completed" ? "鎵撳紑宸ヤ綔鍙? : task.status === "interrupted" ? "浠庢柇鐐圭户缁? : task.status === "failed" ? "浠庡け璐ュ缁х画" : task.pipeline_data ? "缁х画澶勭悊" : "鐢熸垚鑴氭湰"}
          </button>
          {task.status === "running" && <button onClick={async e => { e.stopPropagation(); await window.storybound.cancelTask(task.id); onRefresh(); }}>鍙栨秷</button>}{task.status === "cancelling" && <button disabled>绛夊緟宸叉彁浜ゅ浘鐗?/button>}
          <button title="澶嶅埗浠诲姟" onClick={async e => { e.stopPropagation(); await window.storybound.duplicateTask(task.id); onRefresh(); }}><Copy size={15} /></button>
          <button className="danger" onClick={async e => { e.stopPropagation(); await window.storybound.deleteTask(task.id); onRefresh(); }}>
            <Trash2 size={15} />
          </button>
        </div>
      </article>)}</div>}
    {selected && <TaskDetail task={tasks.find(task => task.id === selected.id) || selected} onClose={() => setSelected(null)} onRefresh={onRefresh} />}
  </section>;
}

function TaskDetail({ task, onClose, onRefresh }: { task: TaskRecord; onClose: () => void; onRefresh: () => void }) {
  const parsePipeline = () => {
    try { return task.pipeline_data ? JSON.parse(task.pipeline_data) as PipelineData : null; }
    catch { return null; }
  };
  const [pipeline, setPipeline] = useState<PipelineData | null>(parsePipeline);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState("");
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
  const [imageUrls, setImageUrls] = useState<Record<number, string>>({});
  const [showRenderOptions, setShowRenderOptions] = useState(false);
  const [renderOptions, setRenderOptions] = useState<RenderTaskOptions>({
    animation: "缂╂斁",
    motionStrength: 0.5,
    forceStaticImages: true,
    burnCaption: true,
    burnTitle: true,
    burnSubtitle: true,
    burnDisclaimer: true,
    autoTextLayout: true,
    titleDuration: 3.2
  });

  useEffect(() => setPipeline(parsePipeline()), [task.pipeline_data]);
  useEffect(() => {
    const paths = pipeline?.scenes?.filter(scene => scene.image_path) || [];
    Promise.all(paths.map(async scene => [scene.index, await window.storybound.pathToDataUrl(scene.image_path!)] as const))
      .then(entries => setImageUrls(Object.fromEntries(entries)));
  }, [task.pipeline_data, pipeline?.scenes?.length]);
  useEffect(() => window.storybound.onTaskEvent(event => {
    if (event.taskId !== task.id) return;
    setMessage(event.message || "浠诲姟澶勭悊涓€?);
    if (event.status !== "running") setBusy("");
  }), [task.id]);

  const applyReturnedTask = async (result: unknown) => {
    if (!result || typeof result !== "object") return;
    const record = result as Partial<TaskRecord>;
    if (!record.pipeline_data) return;
    try {
      const nextPipeline = JSON.parse(record.pipeline_data) as PipelineData;
      setPipeline(nextPipeline);
      const paths = nextPipeline.scenes?.filter(scene => scene.image_path) || [];
      const entries = await Promise.all(paths.map(async scene => [scene.index, await window.storybound.pathToDataUrl(scene.image_path!)] as const));
      setImageUrls(Object.fromEntries(entries));
    } catch {}
  };

  const run = async (label: string, action: () => Promise<unknown>) => {
    setBusy(label);
    setMessage(label.includes("鍚堟垚")
      ? `${label}宸插紑濮嬶紝璇蜂笉瑕侀噸澶嶇偣鍑汇€傛鍦ㄦ湰鍦板鐞嗙幇鏈夊浘鐗囥€侀厤闊冲拰鏂囧瓧鍥惧眰鈥
      : `${label}宸插紑濮嬶紝璇蜂笉瑕侀噸澶嶇偣鍑汇€傛鍦ㄧ瓑寰呭鐞嗙粨鏋溾€);
    try {
      const result = await action();
      await applyReturnedTask(result);
      await onRefresh();
      setMessage(label.includes("鍚堟垚") ? `${label}瀹屾垚锛岃鐐瑰嚮鈥滄挱鏀捐棰戔€濇煡鐪嬫柊鎴愬搧` : `${label}瀹屾垚锛屼换鍔′俊鎭凡鍒锋柊`);
    } catch (error) {
      setMessage(error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error));
    } finally {
      setBusy("");
    }
  };
  const cancelCurrent = async () => {
    setMessage("姝ｅ湪鍙栨秷浠诲姟骞跺仠姝㈠綋鍓嶈姹傗€?);
    try {
      await window.storybound.cancelTask(task.id);
      setBusy("");
      setMessage("浠诲姟宸插彇娑?);
      await onRefresh();
    } catch (error) {
      setMessage(error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error));
    }
  };
  const save = () => pipeline
    ? run("淇濆瓨鑴氭湰", () => window.storybound.updatePipeline(task.id, pipeline))
    : Promise.resolve();
  const updateScene = (index: number, patch: Partial<PipelineScene>) => setPipeline(current => {
    if (!current) return current;
    let invalidatesAudio = false;
    let invalidatesImage = false;
    let invalidatesVideo = false;
    const scenes = current.scenes.map(scene => {
      if (scene.index !== index) return scene;
      const next = { ...scene, ...patch };
      invalidatesAudio = patch.narration !== undefined && patch.narration !== scene.narration;
      invalidatesImage = (patch.image_prompt !== undefined && patch.image_prompt !== scene.image_prompt)
        || (patch.use_reference !== undefined && patch.use_reference !== scene.use_reference);
      invalidatesVideo = invalidatesImage || (patch.visual !== undefined && patch.visual !== scene.visual);
      if (invalidatesAudio) {
        next.audio_path = undefined;
        next.duration = undefined;
        next.audio_status = "pending";
        next.audio_error = undefined;
      }
      if (invalidatesImage) {
        next.image_path = undefined;
        next.image_status = "pending";
        next.image_error = undefined;
        next.image_remote_task_id = undefined;
        next.image_remote_provider = undefined;
      }
      if (invalidatesVideo) {
        next.video_path = undefined;
        next.video_provider = undefined;
        next.video_source_url = undefined;
        next.video_error = undefined;
        next.video_status = "pending";
        next.video_remote_task_id = undefined;
        next.video_remote_model = undefined;
      }
      return next;
    });
    const invalidatesRender = invalidatesAudio || invalidatesImage || invalidatesVideo;
    return {
      ...current,
      narration: scenes.map(scene => scene.narration).join(""),
      scenes,
      runtime: invalidatesRender ? {
        ...(current.runtime || {}),
        current_stage: "review",
        current_step: 3,
        render_status: "pending",
        cover_status: "pending",
        draft_status: "pending",
        final_video: "",
        subtitle_path: "",
        draft_dir: "",
        cover_path: ""
      } : current.runtime
    };
  });
  const missingImageScenes = pipeline?.scenes?.filter(scene =>
    !scene.image_path || ["pending", "failed", "interrupted"].includes(String(scene.image_status || ""))
  ) || [];
  const missingImageCount = missingImageScenes.length;
  const completedImageCount = Math.max(0, Number(pipeline?.scenes?.length || 0) - missingImageCount);
  const hasAssets = Boolean(pipeline?.scenes?.length && pipeline.scenes.every(scene => scene.image_path && scene.audio_path));
  const stages = ["鍒嗘瀽鏂囨", "鏀瑰啓鑴氭湰", "鎷嗗垎鍒嗛暅", "鐢熸垚鐢婚潰", "鐢熸垚閰嶉煶", "鍚堟垚瑙嗛", "鍓槧鑽夌", "瀹屾垚"];

  return <div className="modal-backdrop" onClick={onClose}>
    <div className="task-detail" onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
      <button className="modal-close" onClick={onClose}><X size={18} /></button>
      <span className={`status ${task.status}`}>{statusText(task.status)}</span>
      <h2>{task.title}</h2>
      <p className="detail-source">{task.input_text}</p>
      {task.error_message && <div className="error-box">{task.error_message}</div>}
      {task.current_stage && <div className="checkpoint-box">褰撳墠鏂偣锛歿task.current_stage}{task.last_checkpoint_at ? ` 路 ${new Date(task.last_checkpoint_at).toLocaleString()}` : ""}</div>}
      <div className="stage-strip">{stages.map((stage, index) => <div className={task.current_step >= index + 1 ? "done" : task.status === "review" && index === 3 ? "next" : ""} key={stage}>
        <span>{task.current_step > index ? <CheckCircle2 size={13} /> : index + 1}</span><small>{stage}</small>
      </div>)}</div>
      {(busy || message) && <div className={`workbench-message${busy ? " running" : ""}`}>
        {busy && <LoaderCircle size={15} className="spin" />}
        <span>{message || `${busy}澶勭悊涓€}</span>
      </div>}
      <div className="workflow-actions">
        {!pipeline && <button className="primary" disabled={Boolean(busy)} onClick={() => run("鐢熸垚鑴氭湰", () => window.storybound.prepareTask(task.id))}><Sparkles size={16} />鐢熸垚鑴氭湰涓庡垎闀?/button>}
        {pipeline && <button disabled={Boolean(busy)} onClick={save}><Save size={16} />淇濆瓨淇敼</button>}
        {pipeline && missingImageCount > 0 && <button className="primary" disabled={Boolean(busy)} onClick={async () => {
          await save();
          await run(`琛ラ綈 ${missingImageCount} 寮犵己澶辩敾闈, () => window.storybound.repairMissingImages(task.id));
        }}><RefreshCw size={16} />琛ラ綈缂哄け鐢婚潰锛坽missingImageCount}锛?/button>}
        {pipeline && missingImageCount === 0 && !hasAssets && <button className="primary" disabled={Boolean(busy)} onClick={async () => {
          await save();
          await run("鐢熸垚閰嶉煶涓庤棰?, () => window.storybound.continueTask(task.id));
        }}><Play size={16} />{task.status === "interrupted" ? "浠庢柇鐐圭户缁? : "纭骞剁敓鎴愰厤闊充笌瑙嗛"}</button>}
        {hasAssets && <button className="primary" disabled={Boolean(busy)} onClick={() => setShowRenderOptions(true)}><RefreshCw size={16} />浣跨敤鐜版湁绱犳潗閲嶆柊鍚堟垚</button>}
        {task.status === "cancelling" ? <button className="danger" disabled><LoaderCircle size={16} className="spin" />姝ｅ湪涓嬭浇宸叉彁浜ゅ浘鐗?/button> : (task.status === "running" || Boolean(busy)) && <button className="danger" onClick={cancelCurrent}><X size={16} />鍙栨秷褰撳墠浠诲姟</button>}
      </div>
      <div className="artifact-actions">
        {task.video_path && <button className="primary" onClick={() => window.storybound.openPath(task.video_path!)}><Video size={16} />鎾斁瑙嗛</button>}
        {task.output_dir && <button onClick={() => window.storybound.openPath(task.output_dir!)}><FolderOpen size={16} />鎵撳紑浜х墿鐩綍</button>}
        {task.output_dir && pipeline && <button onClick={() => window.storybound.openPath(`${task.output_dir}/llm-debug`)}><FileText size={16} />鏌ョ湅妯″瀷璇锋眰鏃ュ織</button>}
        {task.output_dir && pipeline && <button onClick={() => window.storybound.openPath(`${task.output_dir}/image-debug`)}><Image size={16} />鏌ョ湅鍥剧墖浠诲姟鏃ュ織</button>}
        {task.draft_dir && <button onClick={() => window.storybound.openPath(task.draft_dir!)}><ExternalLink size={16} />鎵撳紑鍓槧鑽夌</button>}
        {task.cover_path && <button onClick={() => window.storybound.openPath(task.cover_path!)}><Image size={16} />鏌ョ湅灏侀潰娴锋姤</button>}
      </div>
      {pipeline && <div className="publish-result-card">
        <div className="publish-result-head"><div><FileText size={16} /><b>鍙戝竷鏂囨缁撴灉</b></div><span>宸叉寜璧涢亾鎻愮ず璇嶇敓鎴?/span></div>
        <div className="publish-result-grid">
          <div><small>涓绘爣棰?/small><b>{pipeline.title || "鏈敓鎴?}</b></div>
          <div><small>鍓爣棰?/small><b>{pipeline.subtitle?.length ? pipeline.subtitle.join(" / ") : "鏈敓鎴?}</b></div>
        </div>
        <p><b>瑙嗛绠€浠嬶細</b>{pipeline.summary || "鏈敓鎴?}</p>
        {pipeline.tags?.length ? <p><b>鍙戝竷鏍囩锛?/b>{pipeline.tags.join(" ")}</p> : null}
        {pipeline.comments?.length ? <div className="publish-comments"><b>绉嶅瓙璇勮锛?/b>{pipeline.comments.map((item, index) => <span key={`${index}-${item}`}>{index + 1}. {item}</span>)}</div> : null}
      </div>}
      {pipeline?.metadata && <div className="planner-metadata">
        <div className="planner-metadata-head"><div><Sparkles size={16} /><b>澶фā鍨嬭瑙夎鍒掔粨鏋?/b></div><span>{pipeline.metadata.planner_mode === "staged-llm" ? "涓夐樁娈佃鍒? : "鏈湴瑙勫垯"}</span></div>
        <div className="planner-metadata-grid">
          <div><small>鎻愮ず璇嶆ā鏉?/small><b>{pipeline.metadata.template_name || pipeline.metadata.template_id || "璺熼殢璧涢亾"}</b></div>
          <div><small>涓昏妗ｆ</small><b>{String((pipeline.metadata.character_card as any)?.stable_prompt || "鏈彁鍙?)}</b></div>
          <div><small>浜у搧/鍏抽敭鐗╀欢</small><b>{String((pipeline.metadata.product_card as any)?.stable_prompt || "鏈彁鍙?)}</b></div>
          <div><small>鍙傝€冨浘绛栫暐</small><b>{pipeline.metadata.reference_kind || "none"} 路 {pipeline.metadata.reference_available ? "宸蹭笂浼犲弬鑰冨浘" : "鏈笂浼犲弬鑰冨浘"}</b></div>
        </div>
        {pipeline.metadata.visual_continuity?.length ? <p><b>涓€鑷存€ц鍒欙細</b>{pipeline.metadata.visual_continuity.join("锛?)}</p> : null}
      </div>}
      {showRenderOptions && <EditorModal title="浣跨敤鐜版湁绱犳潗閲嶆柊鍚堟垚" saveLabel="寮€濮嬮噸鏂板悎鎴? onClose={() => setShowRenderOptions(false)} onSave={async () => {
        setShowRenderOptions(false);
        if (pipeline) await window.storybound.updatePipeline(task.id, pipeline);
        await run("閲嶆柊鍚堟垚瑙嗛", () => window.storybound.renderTask(task.id, renderOptions));
      }}>
        <div className="rerender-note">涓嶄細閲嶆柊璋冪敤鏂囨銆佸浘鐗囨垨閰嶉煶鎺ュ彛銆傜▼搴忎細鍒犻櫎鏃ф覆鏌撶紦瀛橈紝浣跨敤鐜版湁绱犳潗鐢熸垚 <b>final-rerender.mp4</b>锛屽師鏉ョ殑 final.mp4 浼氫繚鐣欍€?/div>
        <label>鍏ㄩ儴鍥剧墖鍔ㄧ敾
          <select value={renderOptions.animation} onChange={event => setRenderOptions(current => ({ ...current, animation: event.target.value }))}>
            {["浜ゆ浛鎷夐暅", ...IMAGE_ANIMATION_OPTIONS].map(item => <option key={item} value={item}>{item}</option>)}
          </select>
        </label>
        <label>杩愰暅寮哄害锛歿renderOptions.motionStrength.toFixed(2)}
          <input type="range" min="0.25" max="2" step="0.05" value={renderOptions.motionStrength} onChange={event => setRenderOptions(current => ({ ...current, motionStrength: Number(event.target.value) }))} />
        </label>
        <div className="rerender-checks">
          <label><input type="checkbox" checked={renderOptions.forceStaticImages} onChange={event => setRenderOptions(current => ({ ...current, forceStaticImages: event.target.checked }))} />鍏ㄩ儴浣跨敤鐜版湁闈欐€佸浘鐗囷紝涓嶄娇鐢ㄥ浘鐢熻棰戠墖娈?/label>
          <label><input type="checkbox" checked={renderOptions.burnCaption} onChange={event => setRenderOptions(current => ({ ...current, burnCaption: event.target.checked }))} />鐑у綍閫愬彞鏃佺櫧瀛楀箷</label>
          <label><input type="checkbox" checked={renderOptions.burnTitle} onChange={event => setRenderOptions(current => ({ ...current, burnTitle: event.target.checked }))} />鐑у綍鐗囧ご涓绘爣棰?/label>
          <label><input type="checkbox" checked={renderOptions.burnSubtitle} onChange={event => setRenderOptions(current => ({ ...current, burnSubtitle: event.target.checked }))} />鐑у綍鐗囧ご鍓爣棰?/label>
          <label><input type="checkbox" checked={renderOptions.burnDisclaimer} onChange={event => setRenderOptions(current => ({ ...current, burnDisclaimer: event.target.checked }))} />鐑у綍瀹夊叏鎻愮ず/鍏嶈矗澹版槑</label>
          <label><input type="checkbox" checked={renderOptions.autoTextLayout} onChange={event => setRenderOptions(current => ({ ...current, autoTextLayout: event.target.checked }))} />鑷姩閬胯鏂囧瓧锛岄槻姝㈡爣棰樹笌瀛楀箷閲嶅彔</label>
        </div>
        <label>鐗囧ご鏍囬鏄剧ず鏃堕暱锛歿renderOptions.titleDuration.toFixed(1)} 绉?          <input type="range" min="0" max="8" step="0.2" value={renderOptions.titleDuration} onChange={event => setRenderOptions(current => ({ ...current, titleDuration: Number(event.target.value) }))} />
        </label>
        <div className="prompt-safety-note">涓绘爣棰樺拰鍓爣棰樺彧鍦ㄧ墖澶存樉绀猴紱鏃佺櫧瀛楀箷浼氭寜璇箟鎷嗘垚鍗曡鐭彞锛屽苟璺熼殢閰嶉煶閫愭鏄剧ず銆傚浘鐗囧姩鐢婚噰鐢ㄥ壀鏄犻鏍煎叧閿抚銆佽繛缁紦鍏ョ紦鍑轰笌楂樺垎杈ㄧ巼瓒呴噰鏍锋覆鏌擄紱杩愰暅寮哄害鍙敼鍙樺箙搴︼紝涓嶄細鍒囨崲绠楁硶銆?/div>
      </EditorModal>}
      {pipeline?.scenes?.length ? <div className="scene-list">
        <h3>鍒嗛暅宸ヤ綔鍙?路 {pipeline.scenes.length} 闀?路 鍥剧墖宸插畬鎴?{completedImageCount}/{pipeline.scenes.length}{missingImageCount ? ` 路 寰呰ˉ榻?${missingImageCount}` : ""}</h3>
        {pipeline.scenes.map(scene => <div className="scene-editor" key={scene.index}>
          <div className="scene-editor-head">
            <strong>闀滃ご {String(scene.index).padStart(2, "0")}</strong>
            <span>{scene.duration?.toFixed(1) || "鈥?}s</span>
            <div>
              <button
                disabled={Boolean(busy)}
                title={busy && busy !== `閲嶅仛闀滃ご ${scene.index} 鐢婚潰` ? "褰撳墠宸叉湁涓€涓浘鐗囦换鍔℃鍦ㄦ墽琛岋紝璇风瓑寰呭畬鎴愬悗鍐嶆搷浣? : ""}
                onClick={() => run(`閲嶅仛闀滃ご ${scene.index} 鐢婚潰`, () => window.storybound.regenerateScene(task.id, scene.index, "image"))}
              >
                {busy === `閲嶅仛闀滃ご ${scene.index} 鐢婚潰` ? <LoaderCircle size={13} className="spin" /> : <RefreshCw size={13} />}
                {busy === `閲嶅仛闀滃ご ${scene.index} 鐢婚潰` ? "鐢熸垚涓€? : (busy ? "绛夊緟褰撳墠浠诲姟" : (scene.image_path ? "閲嶅仛鐢婚潰" : "鐢熸垚鐢婚潰"))}
              </button>
              <button disabled={Boolean(busy)} onClick={() => run(`鏇挎崲闀滃ご ${scene.index} 鐢婚潰`, () => window.storybound.replaceSceneImage(task.id, scene.index))}><Upload size={13} />涓婁紶鏇挎崲</button>
              <button disabled={Boolean(busy)} onClick={() => run(`閲嶅仛闀滃ご ${scene.index} 閰嶉煶`, () => window.storybound.regenerateScene(task.id, scene.index, "audio"))}><Volume2 size={13} />閲嶅仛閰嶉煶</button>
            </div>
          </div>
          {imageUrls[scene.index] && <div className="scene-preview"><img src={imageUrls[scene.index]} alt={`闀滃ご ${scene.index}`} /></div>}
          <label>鏃佺櫧<textarea value={scene.narration} onChange={e => updateScene(scene.index, { narration: e.target.value })} /></label>
          <label>鐢婚潰鎻忚堪<textarea value={scene.visual} onChange={e => updateScene(scene.index, { visual: e.target.value })} /></label>
          {scene.desc_prompt && <label>{scene.image_prompt_safety_adjusted ? "鍒嗛暅鍩虹鎻愮ず璇嶏紙宸茶嚜鍔ㄦ敼涓哄厠鍒剁敾闈級" : "鍒嗛暅鍩虹鎻愮ず璇嶏紙desc_prompt锛?}<textarea value={scene.desc_prompt} readOnly /></label>}
          <label>鏈€缁堝浘鐗囨彁绀鸿瘝<textarea value={scene.image_prompt} onChange={e => updateScene(scene.index, { image_prompt: e.target.value })} /></label>
          {scene.image_prompt_safety_adjusted && <div className="prompt-safety-note">宸插湪璋冪敤鍥剧墖鎺ュ彛鍓嶈嚜鍔ㄦ浛鎹㈤珮椋庨櫓鐢婚潰銆傛梺鐧藉唴瀹逛繚鎸佷笉鍙橈紝鍥剧墖鍙〃鐜板寘鎵庡悗鐨勭姸鎬併€佷汉鐗╃鎯呬笌鐜锛屼笉鍐嶅彂閫佺洿鎺ョ殑鍙椾激缁嗚妭銆?/div>}
          <div className="scene-reference-control">
            <label><input type="checkbox" checked={Boolean(scene.use_reference)} onChange={e => updateScene(scene.index, { use_reference: e.target.checked })} />鏈暅浣跨敤涓昏/浜у搧鍙傝€冨浘</label>
            <span>{scene.reference_reason || "澶фā鍨嬫湭鎻愪緵鍒ゆ柇璇存槑"}</span>
            {scene.subject_presence && <em>涓讳綋锛歿scene.subject_presence}</em>}
          </div>
          <div className="scene-assets">
            <span className={scene.image_path ? "ready" : ""}>鐢婚潰 {scene.image_path ? "宸茬敓鎴? : "鏈敓鎴?}{scene.image_status ? ` 路 ${assetStatusText(scene.image_status)}` : ""}</span>
            <span className={scene.audio_path ? "ready" : ""}>閰嶉煶 {scene.audio_path ? "宸茬敓鎴? : "鏈敓鎴?}{scene.audio_status ? ` 路 ${assetStatusText(scene.audio_status)}` : ""}</span>
            {scene.image_error && <span className="asset-warning" title={scene.image_error}>鐢婚潰閿欒锛歿scene.image_error}</span>}
            {scene.audio_error && <span className="asset-warning" title={scene.audio_error}>閰嶉煶閿欒锛歿scene.audio_error}</span>}
            {scene.image_provider && <span className="ready">{imageProviderName(scene.image_provider)}</span>}
            {scene.use_reference && <span className="ready">浣跨敤鍙傝€冨浘</span>}
            {scene.video_path && <span className="ready">鍔ㄦ€佺敾闈㈠凡鐢熸垚</span>}
            {scene.video_provider && <span className="ready">{scene.video_provider}</span>}
            {scene.video_error && <span className="asset-warning" title={scene.video_error}>鍔ㄦ€佸厹搴曪細{scene.video_error}</span>}
            {scene.source_url && <button onClick={() => window.open(scene.source_url, "_blank")}>绱犳潗鏉ユ簮</button>}
            {scene.video_source_url && <button onClick={() => window.open(scene.video_source_url, "_blank")}>鍔ㄦ€佹潵婧?/button>}
          </div>
        </div>)}
      </div> : <div className="detail-empty">鍏堢敓鎴愯剼鏈笌鍒嗛暅锛屽湪纭鍐呭鍚庡啀娑堣€楁椂闂寸敓鎴愬浘鐗囥€侀厤闊冲拰瑙嗛銆?/div>}
    </div>
  </div>;
}


const VOICE_FAVORITES_KEY = "storybound.voiceFavorites.v2";
const VOICE_SELECTION_KEY = "storybound.lastVoiceSelection.v2";
const DEFAULT_VOICE_FAVORITES = [
  "zh_male_dongfanghaoran_moon_bigtts",
  "zh_male_yuanboxiaoshu_moon_bigtts",
  "zh_female_wenrouxiaoya_moon_bigtts",
  "zh_female_shuangkuaisisi_moon_bigtts"
];

function readVoiceFavorites() {
  try {
    const parsed = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(VOICE_FAVORITES_KEY) || "null");
    return Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed.filter(item => typeof item === "string").slice(0, 5) : [...DEFAULT_VOICE_FAVORITES];
  } catch {
    return [...DEFAULT_VOICE_FAVORITES];
  }
}

function VolcVoicePicker({ open, value, speed, configured, onClose, onPick }: {
  open: boolean;
  value: string;
  speed: number;
  configured: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onPick: (voice: VolcVoiceOption) => void;
}) {
  const selectedVoice = VOLC_VOICES.find(item => item.id === value);
  const [version, setVersion] = useState<"1.0" | "2.0">(selectedVoice?.version || "2.0");
  const [category, setCategory] = useState(selectedVoice?.category || "narration");
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [favorites, setFavorites] = useState<string[]>(readVoiceFavorites);
  const [loadingId, setLoadingId] = useState("");
  const [playingId, setPlayingId] = useState("");
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
  const audioRef = useRef<HTMLAudioElement | null>(null);

  const stopAudio = () => {
    if (audioRef.current) {
      audioRef.current.pause();
      audioRef.current.src = "";
    }
    setPlayingId("");
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const voice = VOLC_VOICES.find(item => item.id === value);
    setVersion(voice?.version || "2.0");
    setCategory(voice?.category || "narration");
    setQuery("");
    setMessage("");
    return stopAudio;
  }, [open, value]);

  useEffect(() => {
    try { localStorage.setItem(VOICE_FAVORITES_KEY, JSON.stringify(favorites)); } catch {}
  }, [favorites]);

  const categories = useMemo(() => [
    { id: "favorites", name: "鎴戠殑鏀惰棌", hint: `宸叉敹钘?${favorites.filter(id => VOLC_VOICES.some(item => item.id === id && item.version === version)).length} 涓猔 },
    ...VOLC_VOICE_CATEGORIES.filter(cat => VOLC_VOICES.some(item => item.version === version && item.category === cat.id))
  ], [favorites, version]);

  const visibleVoices = useMemo(() => {
    const needle = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    return VOLC_VOICES.filter(item => {
      if (item.version !== version) return false;
      if (category === "favorites") {
        if (!favorites.includes(item.id)) return false;
      } else if (item.category !== category) return false;
      return !needle || item.name.toLowerCase().includes(needle) || item.tag.toLowerCase().includes(needle) || item.id.toLowerCase().includes(needle);
    });
  }, [version, category, query, favorites]);

  const toggleFavorite = (id: string) => {
    setFavorites(current => current.includes(id)
      ? current.filter(item => item !== id)
      : current.length >= 5 ? current : [...current, id]);
  };

  const previewVoice = async (voice: VolcVoiceOption) => {
    setMessage("");
    if (playingId === voice.id) {
      stopAudio();
      return;
    }
    if (!configured) {
      setMessage("璇峰厛鍦ㄢ€滆缃?鈫?TTS 閰嶉煶 鈫?鐏北寮曟搸鈥濆～鍐?App ID 鍜?Access Token");
      return;
    }
    stopAudio();
    setLoadingId(voice.id);
    try {
      const result = await window.storybound.previewVolcVoice({ speaker: voice.id, speed, text: "浠栨潵鍒版睙鍗椾竴涓皬鏉戝簞" });
      const audio = new Audio(result.dataUrl);
      audioRef.current = audio;
      audio.addEventListener("ended", () => setPlayingId(""), { once: true });
      audio.addEventListener("error", () => { setPlayingId(""); setMessage("璇曞惉闊抽鎾斁澶辫触"); }, { once: true });
      await audio.play();
      setPlayingId(voice.id);
      setMessage(result.cached ? "宸蹭粠鏈湴璇曞惉缂撳瓨鎾斁" : "璇曞惉宸茬敓鎴愬苟淇濆瓨鍒版湰鍦帮紝涓嬫鍙洿鎺ユ挱鏀?);
    } catch (error) {
      setMessage(error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error));
    } finally {
      setLoadingId("");
    }
  };

  if (!open) return null;
  return <div className="voice-picker-overlay" onMouseDown={event => event.target === event.currentTarget && onClose()}>
    <div className="voice-picker-modal" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="閫夋嫨鐏北闊宠壊">
      <header className="voice-picker-head"><div><b>閫夋嫨闊宠壊</b><small>鍏?{VOLC_VOICES.length} 涓伀灞遍煶鑹?/small></div><button onClick={() => { stopAudio(); onClose(); }}><X size={16} /></button></header>
      <div className="voice-picker-version-row">
        {(["2.0", "1.0"] as const).map(item => <button key={item} data-active={version === item || undefined} onClick={() => { setVersion(item); setCategory("narration"); stopAudio(); }}><b>璇煶鍚堟垚 {item}</b><small>{item === "2.0" ? "鏂扮増 路 鎯呮劅鏇磋嚜鐒? : "缁忓吀鐗?路 闊宠壊鏇村"}</small></button>)}
      </div>
      {!configured && <div className="voice-picker-banner">璇曞惉闇€瑕佸厛閰嶇疆鐏北 TTS 鍑瘉銆傛湭閰嶇疆鏃朵粛鍙€夋嫨闊宠壊锛屼絾涓嶈兘鐢熸垚璇曞惉銆?/div>}
      <div className="voice-picker-toolbar"><Search size={14} /><input value={query} onChange={event => setQuery(event.target.value)} placeholder="鎸夊悕瀛椼€佹爣绛炬垨闊宠壊 ID 鎼滅储" /><span>棣栨璇曞惉璋冪敤鎺ュ彛锛屼箣鍚庤鍙栨湰鍦扮紦瀛?/span></div>
      <div className="voice-picker-body">
        <aside className="voice-picker-categories">{categories.map(item => <button key={item.id} data-active={category === item.id || undefined} onClick={() => { setCategory(item.id); stopAudio(); }}><b>{item.name}</b><small>{item.hint}</small></button>)}</aside>
        <main className="voice-picker-list">{visibleVoices.length ? visibleVoices.map(voice => {
          const selected = voice.id === value;
          const favorite = favorites.includes(voice.id);
          const loading = loadingId === voice.id;
          const playing = playingId === voice.id;
          return <article key={voice.id} data-selected={selected || undefined}>
            <div className="voice-picker-info"><b><Volume2 size={13} />{voice.name}<em>{voice.version}</em></b><span>{voice.tag}</span><small>{voice.id}</small></div>
            <div className="voice-picker-actions">
              <button className="voice-favorite-btn" title={favorite ? "鍙栨秷鏀惰棌" : "鏀惰棌闊宠壊"} onClick={() => toggleFavorite(voice.id)}>{favorite ? "鈽? : "鈽?}</button>
              <button disabled={loading} onClick={() => previewVoice(voice)}>{loading ? <LoaderCircle size={13} className="spin" /> : playing ? <X size={13} /> : <Play size={13} />}{loading ? "鍚堟垚涓? : playing ? "鍋滄" : "璇曞惉"}</button>
              <button className={selected ? "primary" : ""} onClick={() => { onPick(voice); stopAudio(); onClose(); }}>{selected ? "宸查€? : "閫夌敤"}</button>
            </div>
          </article>;
        }) : <div className="voice-picker-empty">娌℃湁鍖归厤鐨勯煶鑹?/div>}</main>
      </div>
      <footer className="voice-picker-footer"><span>{message || "璇曞惉鏂囨湰锛氫粬鏉ュ埌姹熷崡涓€涓皬鏉戝簞"}</span><button onClick={() => { stopAudio(); onClose(); }}>鍏抽棴</button></footer>
    </div>
  </div>;
}

function CreateTask({ onCreated, onNotice, onManageTemplates, onManageStyles }: {
  onCreated: (started: boolean) => void | Promise<void>;
  onNotice: (message: string) => void;
  onManageTemplates: () => void;
  onManageStyles: () => void;
}) {
  const [track, setTrack] = useState("character-story");
  const [title, setTitle] = useState("");
  const [text, setText] = useState("");
  const trackStyles: Record<string, string> = {
    "character-story": "black-white", "health-book": "oil-painting", "culture-knowledge": "ancient-cinematic",
    "picture-book": "pixar-3d", ecommerce: "realistic", inspiration: "cinematic",
    "folk-tale": "folk-tale-gongbi", general: "realistic", "food-vlog": "vintage-film"
  };
  const visualStyles = BUILTIN_VISUAL_STYLES.map(item => [item.id, item.name, item.tag] as const);
  const [style, setStyle] = useState(trackStyles["character-story"]);
  const [ratio, setRatio] = useState("9:16");
  const [targetScenes, setTargetScenes] = useState(0);
  const [targetLength, setTargetLength] = useState(0);
  const [ttsSpeed, setTtsSpeed] = useState(1);
  const [speaker, setSpeaker] = useState(DEFAULT_VOLC_VOICE_ID);
  const [voicePickerOpen, setVoicePickerOpen] = useState(false);
  const [volcPreviewConfigured, setVolcPreviewConfigured] = useState(false);
  const [ttsProvider, setTtsProvider] = useState("system");
  const [systemVoices, setSystemVoices] = useState<SystemVoice[]>([]);
  const [taskType, setTaskType] = useState("story");
  const [podcastImageMode, setPodcastImageMode] = useState("multi");
  const [podcastSpeakers, setPodcastSpeakers] = useState("mizai-dayi");
  const [rewriteIntensity, setRewriteIntensity] = useState("standard");
  const [narrativePov, setNarrativePov] = useState("original");
  const [keepPromotion, setKeepPromotion] = useState(false);
  const [materialSource, setMaterialSource] = useState("ai");
  const [videoIntro, setVideoIntro] = useState(0);
  const [customIntroCount, setCustomIntroCount] = useState(5);
  const [videoIntroDuration, setVideoIntroDuration] = useState(0);
  const [templateId, setTemplateId] = useState("default-portrait-9-16");
  const [referenceImagePath, setReferenceImagePath] = useState("");
  const [productReferenceImagePath, setProductReferenceImagePath] = useState("");
  const [characterConsistencyMode, setCharacterConsistencyMode] = useState<"off" | "upload" | "auto">("off");
  const [coverImageMode, setCoverImageMode] = useState("off");
  const [coverTemplateId, setCoverTemplateId] = useState("cinematic-poster");
  const [processingMode, setProcessingMode] = useState("auto");
  const [pauseMode, setPauseMode] = useState("script");
  const [pausePoints, setPausePoints] = useState<number[]>([]);
  const [advancedOpen, setAdvancedOpen] = useState(false);
  const [sourceMode, setSourceMode] = useState("paste");
  const [sourceQuery, setSourceQuery] = useState("");
  const [sourceRequirements, setSourceRequirements] = useState("");
  const [researchWeb, setResearchWeb] = useState(true);
  const [researchAi, setResearchAi] = useState(false);
  const [researchIma, setResearchIma] = useState(false);
  const [researching, setResearching] = useState(false);
  const [promptTemplateId, setPromptTemplateId] = useState("character-story");
  const [customStyles, setCustomStyles] = useState<StyleRecord[]>([]);
  const [promptTemplates, setPromptTemplates] = useState<PromptTemplateRecord[]>([]);
  const [draftTemplates, setDraftTemplates] = useState<DraftTemplate[]>([]);
  const [coverTemplates, setCoverTemplates] = useState<CoverTemplate[]>([]);
  const [bgmItems, setBgmItems] = useState<BgmRecord[]>([]);
  const [bgmId, setBgmId] = useState("builtin");
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [draftJustSaved, setDraftJustSaved] = useState(false);
  const [voiceSelectionLoaded, setVoiceSelectionLoaded] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    window.storybound.listStyles().then(setCustomStyles);
    window.storybound.listPromptTemplates().then(setPromptTemplates);
    window.storybound.getTemplates().then(setDraftTemplates);
    window.storybound.listCoverTemplates().then(setCoverTemplates);
    window.storybound.listBgm().then(setBgmItems);
    Promise.all([window.storybound.getConfig(), window.storybound.listTasks()]).then(([config, tasks]) => {
      const latestTask = tasks.find(item => item.task_type !== "podcast");
      let remembered: { provider?: string; speaker?: string; speed?: number } | null = null;
      try {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(VOICE_SELECTION_KEY) || "null");
        if (parsed && typeof parsed === "object") remembered = parsed;
      } catch {
        remembered = null;
      }

      const configProvider = config.tts?.provider === "volcengine" ? "volcengine" : "system";
      const latestProvider = latestTask?.tts_provider === "system" && String(latestTask?.speaker || "").includes("_bigtts")
        ? "volcengine"
        : latestTask?.tts_provider;
      const rememberedProvider = remembered?.provider;
      // 璁剧疆椤靛喅瀹氬綋鍓嶄娇鐢ㄥ摢涓€绉?TTS 鏈嶅姟锛涙湰鍦拌蹇嗗彧鎭㈠璇ユ湇鍔′笅鏈€鍚庨€変腑鐨勯煶鑹插拰璇€熴€?      const provider = configProvider;
      const configSpeaker = provider === "system"
        ? (config.tts?.system?.voice || "")
        : (config.tts?.volcengine?.speaker || DEFAULT_VOLC_VOICE_ID);
      const latestSpeaker = latestProvider === provider && typeof latestTask?.speaker === "string" ? latestTask.speaker : undefined;
      const rememberedSpeaker = rememberedProvider === provider && typeof remembered?.speaker === "string" ? remembered.speaker : undefined;

      setTtsProvider(provider);
      setSpeaker(rememberedSpeaker ?? latestSpeaker ?? configSpeaker);
      setTtsSpeed(Number(remembered?.speed || latestTask?.tts_speed || 1));
      setVolcPreviewConfigured(Boolean(config.tts?.volcengine?.app_id?.trim() && config.tts?.volcengine?.access_key?.trim()));
      setVoiceSelectionLoaded(true);
    }).catch(() => setVoiceSelectionLoaded(true));
    window.storybound.listSystemVoices().then(setSystemVoices).catch(() => setSystemVoices([]));
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!voiceSelectionLoaded) return;
    localStorage.setItem(VOICE_SELECTION_KEY, JSON.stringify({ provider: ttsProvider, speaker, speed: ttsSpeed }));
  }, [voiceSelectionLoaded, ttsProvider, speaker, ttsSpeed]);

  const chooseTrack = (id: string) => {
    setTrack(id);
    setStyle(trackStyles[id] || "realistic");
    setPromptTemplateId(id);
  };
  const chooseTemplate = (id: string) => {
    setTemplateId(id);
    const selected = draftTemplates.find(item => item.id === id);
    if (!selected) return;
    const config = JSON.parse(selected.config);
    setRatio(config.image?.ratio || config.canvas?.ratio || "9:16");
    if (config.audio?.defaultBgmId) setBgmId(config.audio.defaultBgmId);
  };

  const effectivePromptTemplateId = promptTemplateId || track;
  const selectedPromptTemplate = systemPromptTemplates.find(item => item.id === effectivePromptTemplateId)
    || promptTemplates.find(item => item.id === effectivePromptTemplateId);
  const selectedReferenceKind = String(selectedPromptTemplate?.reference_kind || "character");
  const productReferenceMode = selectedReferenceKind === "product";

  const normalizedProcessingMode = processingMode === "semi" ? "semi_auto" : processingMode;
  const pauseStageOptions = [
    { value: 3, label: "鑴氭湰涓庡垎闀滅‘璁? },
    { value: 4, label: "鍥剧墖纭" },
    { value: 5, label: "閰嶉煶纭" }
  ];
  const disabledPauseSteps: number[] = [];
  const effectivePausePoints = pausePoints.filter(step => !disabledPauseSteps.includes(step));
  const willPauseAtScript = pauseMode === "script" || (pauseMode === "custom" && effectivePausePoints.includes(3));
  const canSave = text.trim().length > 0;
  const canStart = text.trim().length >= 50;
  const wordsPerScene = targetLength > 0 && targetScenes > 0 ? Math.round(targetLength / targetScenes) : 0;
  const currentSceneCharacters = Array.from(text.trim().replace(/\s+/gu, "")).length;
  const estimatedCharsPerMinute = 230 * Math.max(0.75, Math.min(1.5, Number(ttsSpeed || 1)));
  const estimatedDurationMinutes = currentSceneCharacters / estimatedCharsPerMinute;
  const autoSceneEstimate = Math.max(1, Math.round(estimatedDurationMinutes * 5));

  const submit = async (start: boolean) => {
    const cleanText = text.trim();
    if (!cleanText) {
      onNotice("璇峰厛濉啓鏂囨鍐呭");
      return;
    }
    if (start && cleanText.length < 50) {
      onNotice("鏂囨鑷冲皯闇€瑕?50 涓瓧锛屽綋鍓嶅唴瀹硅繃鐭?);
      return;
    }
    if (taskType === "podcast" && normalizedProcessingMode !== "auto") {
      const podcastLines = cleanText.split(/\r?\n/).map(line => line.trim()).filter(Boolean);
      const allTagged = podcastLines.length > 0 && podcastLines.every(line => /^[\[銆怾\s*[ABab]\s*[\]銆慮\s*.+/.test(line));
      if (!allTagged) {
        onNotice("鍙屼汉鎾浣跨敤鍗婅嚜鍔ㄦ垨鐩存帴鍑虹墖鏃讹紝姣忎竴琛岄兘蹇呴』浠?[A] 鎴?[B] 寮€澶?);
        return;
      }
    }

    setSaving(true);
    setDraftJustSaved(false);
    try {
      const effectivePausePointsResolved = pauseMode === "image" ? [4]
        : pauseMode === "audio" ? [5]
        : pauseMode === "custom" ? effectivePausePoints
        : [];
      const effectivePause = pauseMode === "script"
        ? "script"
        : pauseMode === "image" || pauseMode === "audio"
          ? "custom"
          : pauseMode === "custom"
            ? (effectivePausePointsResolved.includes(3) ? "script" : effectivePausePointsResolved.length ? "custom" : "none")
            : "none";
      const effectiveVideoIntro = materialSource === "ai" && taskType !== "podcast"
        ? (videoIntro === -2 ? Math.max(1, customIntroCount) : videoIntro) : 0;
      const effectiveCoverMode = materialSource === "ai" ? coverImageMode : "off";
      const task = await window.storybound.createTask({
        title: title.trim() || cleanText.slice(0, 18),
        inputText: cleanText,
        track, style, ratio,
        targetScenes: targetScenes || undefined,
        targetLength: normalizedProcessingMode === "auto" ? (targetLength || undefined) : undefined,
        ttsSpeed, promptTemplateId,
        rewriteIntensity: normalizedProcessingMode === "auto" ? rewriteIntensity : "standard",
        narrativePov: normalizedProcessingMode === "auto" ? narrativePov : "original",
        keepPromotion: normalizedProcessingMode === "auto" ? keepPromotion : true,
        materialSource, templateId,
        referenceImagePath: materialSource === "ai" && !productReferenceMode ? referenceImagePath : "",
        productReferenceImagePath: materialSource === "ai" && productReferenceMode ? productReferenceImagePath : "",
        characterConsistencyMode: materialSource === "ai" && !productReferenceMode ? characterConsistencyMode : "off",
        coverImageMode: effectiveCoverMode, coverTemplateId,
        pauseMode: effectivePause,
        sourceMode, sourceQuery, sourceRequirements, bgmId, speaker, ttsProvider,
        taskType, scriptFormat: taskType === "podcast" ? "dialogue" : "narration",
        podcastImageMode, podcastSpeakers,
        processingMode: normalizedProcessingMode,
        pausePoints: effectivePausePointsResolved,
        videoIntro: effectiveVideoIntro,
        videoIntroDuration: effectiveVideoIntro === 0 ? 0 : videoIntroDuration,
        researchWeb, researchAi, researchIma
      });

      await onCreated(start);
      if (start) {
        const action = effectivePause === "script" ? window.storybound.prepareTask(task.id) : window.storybound.runTask(task.id);
        void action.catch(error => onNotice(error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)));
      } else {
        setDraftJustSaved(true);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      onNotice(error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error));
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (event.ctrlKey && event.key === "Enter" && canStart && !saving) {
        event.preventDefault();
        void submit(true);
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
  });

  return <section className="create-page-root">
    <div className="create-page-heading">
      <div className="create-page-heading-icon"><WandSparkles size={18} /></div>
      <div><h1>鍒涘缓瑙嗛浠诲姟</h1><p>绮樿创涓€娈典汉鐗╂晠浜嬶紝鍑犲垎閽熷悗鍦ㄥ壀鏄犻噷鎵撳紑</p></div>
    </div>
    <div className="home-create">
      <CreateSection title="鏂囨" desc="鏀瑰啓 路 鍙欎簨瑙嗚 路 鐩爣瀛楁暟">
        <label className="field-title">鏍囬 <span>鍙€?/span><input value={title} onChange={e => setTitle(e.target.value)} placeholder="鐣欑┖浼氫粠鏂囨鑷姩鎻愬彇" /></label>
        <div className="source-tabs">
          <button data-active={sourceMode === "paste"} onClick={() => setSourceMode("paste")}><b>绮樿创鏂囨</b><span>宸叉湁瀵规爣鏂囨锛岀洿鎺ヨ创杩涙潵鏀瑰啓</span></button>
          <button data-active={sourceMode === "research"} onClick={() => setSourceMode("research")}><b>AI 鍒涗綔 <em>NEW</em></b><span>杈撳叆鍏抽敭璇嶏紝AI 鑷姩鎼滆祫鏂欏苟鍒涗綔鍘熺</span></button>
        </div>
        {sourceMode === "research" ? <div className="research-box">
          <label>鍏抽敭璇?input value={sourceQuery} onChange={e => setSourceQuery(e.target.value)} placeholder="渚嬪锛氶挶瀛︽．鍥炲浗 / 寮犳姊?/ 鑻规灉绉嬪鍙戝竷浼? /></label>
          <div><strong>鏁版嵁婧?<small>鑷冲皯閫変竴涓?/small></strong>

"馃幍 鍐呯疆 BGM" : item.name} />)}<button className="choice-chip" onClick={async () => { const added = await window.storybound.addBgm(); if (added) { setBgmItems(await window.storybound.listBgm()); setBgmId(added.id); } }}><Plus size={14} />娣诲姞</button></OptionGroup>
      </CreateSection>

      <div className="advanced-block">
        <button className="advanced-trigger" onClick={() => setAdvancedOpen(!advancedOpen)}>{advancedOpen ? "鈻? : "鈻?} <span><b>楂樼骇閫夐」</b><small>澶勭悊妯″紡 路 鏆傚仠纭锛堥粯璁ゅ€奸€氬父宸茶冻澶燂級</small></span></button>
        {advancedOpen && <div className="advanced-content">
          <div className="advanced-two-column">
            <OptionGroup title="澶勭悊妯″紡" hint="鍚庣画鎻愮ず璇嶃€佺敓鍥俱€侀厤闊冲拰鍓槧鑽夌閮戒細缁х画鎵ц"><Choice active={normalizedProcessingMode === "auto"} onClick={() => setProcessingMode("auto")} title="鍏ㄨ嚜鍔? badge="鎺ㄨ崘" /><Choice active={normalizedProcessingMode === "semi_auto"} onClick={() => setProcessingMode("semi_auto")} title="鍗婅嚜鍔? /><Choice active={normalizedProcessingMode === "direct"} onClick={() => setProcessingMode("direct")} title="鐩存帴鍑虹墖" /></OptionGroup>
            <OptionGroup title="鏆傚仠纭" hint="鍙繚鐣欑湡瀹炲彲鐢ㄧ殑涓変釜纭鑺傜偣锛岄伩鍏嶈瀵?><Choice active={pauseMode === "none"} onClick={() => setPauseMode("none")} title="涓嶆殏鍋? /><Choice active={pauseMode === "script"} onClick={() => setPauseMode("script")} title="鑴氭湰涓庡垎闀滅‘璁? badge="鎺ㄨ崘" /><Choice active={pauseMode === "image"} onClick={() => setPauseMode("image")} title="鍥剧墖纭" /><Choice active={pauseMode === "audio"} onClick={() => setPauseMode("audio")} title="閰嶉煶纭" /><Choice active={pauseMode === "custom"} onClick={() => setPauseMode("custom")} title="鑷畾涔? /></OptionGroup>
          </div>
          {pauseMode === "custom" && <div className="pause-points"><b>鍕鹃€夆€滃摢浜涢樁娈靛悗鏆傚仠璁╂垜纭鈥?/b>{pauseStageOptions.map(item => <label key={item.value}><input type="checkbox" checked={effectivePausePoints.includes(item.value)} onChange={e => setPausePoints(current => e.target.checked ? [...current.filter(step => step !== item.value), item.value] : current.filter(step => step !== item.value))} />Step {item.value} 路 {item.label}</label>)}</div>}
          {normalizedProcessingMode !== "auto" && <div className="mode-note">{normalizedProcessingMode === "semi_auto" ? "鍗婅嚜鍔細鍘熸枃閫愬瓧閿佸畾锛汣laude 鍙粍鍚堣繛缁師鏂囩紪鍙风‘瀹氬垎闀滐紝骞剁敓鎴愪富瑙?浜у搧妗ｆ鍜岀敾闈㈡彁绀鸿瘝銆? : "鐩存帴鍑虹墖锛氳烦杩囬瀹″拰鏀瑰啓锛屾寜鑷劧璇箟鍙婃瘡鍒嗛挓绾?5 闀滅殑鑺傚鑷姩鍒囧垎銆?}</div>}
          <label>鎻愮ず璇嶆ā鏉?select value={promptTemplateId} onChange={e => setPromptTemplateId(e.target.value)}><option value="">璺熼殢璧涢亾榛樿</option><optgroup label="绯荤粺妯℃澘">{systemPromptTemplates.map(item => <option key={item.id} value={item.id}>{item.name}</option>)}</optgroup>{promptTemplates.length > 0 && <optgroup label="鑷畾涔夋ā鏉?>{promptTemplates.map(item => <option key={item.id} value={item.id}>{item.name}</option>)}</optgroup>}</select><small>鍏ㄨ嚜鍔ㄤ笌鍗婅嚜鍔ㄤ細鎵ц鏂囨/鍏冩暟鎹?鍒嗛暅涓夐樁娈佃鍒掞紱鐩存帴鍑虹墖涓嶈皟鐢ㄥぇ妯″瀷銆?/small></label>
        </div>}
      </div>
      <div className="create-submit-bar"><div><b>鑷畾涔?/ 鍏朵粬</b><small>{canStart ? "褰撳墠璇█妯″瀷 路 鍙紑濮嬬敓鎴? : canSave ? `杩橀渶 ${Math.max(0, 50 - text.trim().length)} 瀛楁墠鑳藉紑濮嬬敓鎴恅 : "璇疯緭鍏ヨ嚦灏?50 瀛楁枃妗?}</small></div><button disabled={!canSave || saving} onClick={() => submit(false)}>{draftJustSaved ? <CheckCircle2 size={15} /> : <Save size={15} />}{draftJustSaved ? "宸蹭繚瀛? : "淇濆瓨涓鸿崏绋?}</button><button className="primary" disabled={!canStart || saving} onClick={() => submit(true)}>{saving ? <LoaderCircle className="spin" size={17} /> : <Sparkles size={17} />}{willPauseAtScript ? "鐢熸垚鑴氭湰涓庡垎闀? : "鐩存帴寮€濮嬬敓鎴?} <kbd>Ctrl+Enter</kbd></button></div>
    </div>
    <VolcVoicePicker open={voicePickerOpen} value={speaker} speed={ttsSpeed} configured={volcPreviewConfigured} onClose={() => setVoicePickerOpen(false)} onPick={voice => setSpeaker(voice.id)} />
  </section>;
}

function CreateSection({ title, desc, children }: { title: string; desc: string; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const Icon = title === "鏂囨" ? FileText : title === "鍑哄浘" ? Image : title === "灏侀潰娴锋姤" ? Sparkles : Mic2;
  return <div className="create-section">
    <header><span className="create-section-icon"><Icon size={16} /></span><div><h2>{title}</h2><span>{desc}</span></div></header>
    <div className="create-section-body">{children}</div>
  </div>;
}

function OptionGroup({ title, hint, children }: { title: string; hint?: string; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return <div className="option-group"><div className="option-label"><b>{title}</b>{hint && <small>{hint}</small>}</div><div className="choice-row">{children}</div></div>;
}

function StyleChoice({ id, active, onClick, title, desc, previewSrc }: {
  id: string; active: boolean; onClick: () => void; title: string; desc?: string; previewSrc?: string;
}) {
  return <span className="style-choice-wrap" data-style-id={id}>
    <button type="button" className="choice-chip style-choice-chip" data-active={active || undefined} onClick={onClick}>
      <b>{title}</b>{desc && <small>路 {desc}</small>}
    </button>
    {previewSrc && <span className="style-hover-card" role="tooltip">
      <img src={previewSrc} alt={`${title}椋庢牸棰勮`} loading="lazy" />
      <b>{title}</b>
    </span>}
  </span>;
}

function Choice({ active, onClick, title, desc, badge, disabled = false }: { active: boolean; onClick: () => void; title: string; desc?: string; badge?: string; disabled?: boolean }) {
  return <button type="button" className="choice-chip" data-active={active || undefined} disabled={disabled} onClick={onClick}><b>{title}</b>{badge && <em>{badge}</em>}{desc && <small>路 {desc}</small>}</button>;
}

function SettingsPage({ config, onSave }: { config: AppConfig; onSave: (config: AppConfig) => void }) {
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState(config);
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<SettingsTab>("llm");
  const setSection = <K extends keyof AppConfig>(section: K, value: AppConfig[K]) =>
    setDraft({ ...draft, [section]: value });

  return <section>
    <PageHeader eyebrow="PREFERENCES" title="璁剧疆" desc="閰嶇疆璇█妯″瀷銆佸閮ㄧ敓鍥俱€佹湰鏈?鐏北閰嶉煶銆佸壀鏄犺崏绋夸笌鏈湴璺緞銆?
      actions={<button className="primary" onClick={() => onSave(draft)}>淇濆瓨璁剧疆</button>} />
    <div className="settings-grid">
      <div className="settings-nav">
        <SettingsNavButton active={tab === "llm"} onClick={() => setTab("llm")} icon={Sparkles} label="璇█妯″瀷" />
        <SettingsNavButton active={tab === "image"} onClick={() => setTab("image")} icon={Image} label="鍥剧墖鐢熸垚" />
        <SettingsNavButton active={tab === "tts"} onClick={() => setTab("tts")} icon={Mic2} label="TTS 閰嶉煶" />
        <SettingsNavButton active={tab === "output"} onClick={() => setTab("output")} icon={Clapperboard} label="瑙嗛涓庤崏绋? />
        <SettingsNavButton active={tab === "diagnostics"} onClick={() => setTab("diagnostics")} icon={CircleHelp} label="鍏充簬涓庤瘖鏂? />
      </div>
      <div className="settings-panel">
        {tab === "llm" && <LlmSettings draft={draft} setDraft={setDraft} />}
        {tab === "image" && <ImageSettings draft={draft} setDraft={setDraft} />}
        {tab === "tts" && <TtsSettings draft={draft} setDraft={setDraft} />}
        {tab === "output" && <OutputSettings draft={draft} setSection={setSection} />}
        {tab === "diagnostics" && <DiagnosticsPage />}
      </div>
    </div>
  </section>;
}

function SettingsNavButton({ active, onClick, icon: Icon, label }: {
  active: boolean; onClick: () => void; icon: typeof Sparkles; label: string;
}) {
  return <button className={active ? "active" : ""} onClick={onClick}><Icon size={17} />{label}<ChevronRight size={15} /></button>;
}

function PanelHeading({ icon: Icon, title, desc }: { icon: typeof Sparkles; title: string; desc: string }) {
  return <div className="panel-heading"><div className="panel-icon"><Icon size={21} /></div><div><h3>{title}</h3><p>{desc}</p></div></div>;
}

function LlmSettings({ draft, setDraft }: { draft: AppConfig; setDraft: (next: AppConfig) => void }) {
  const [testMessage, setTestMessage] = useState("");
  const [profiles, setProfiles] = useState<LlmProfile[]>([]);
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<Partial<LlmProfile> | null>(null);
  const [showKey, setShowKey] = useState(false);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const refresh = async () => setProfiles(await window.storybound.listLlmProfiles());
  useEffect(() => { refresh(); }, []);

  const startCreate = () => {
    setTestMessage("");
    setShowKey(false);
    setEditing({ name: "", provider: "custom", protocol: "openai", api_key: "", model: "", base_url: "", proxy_url: "", is_default: profiles.length ? 0 : 1 });
  };
  const activate = async (profile: LlmProfile) => {
    const active = await window.storybound.activateLlmProfile(profile.id);
    setDraft({ ...draft, llm: { ...draft.llm, provider: active.provider, protocol: active.protocol, base_url: active.base_url, api_key: active.api_key, model: active.model, proxy_url: active.proxy_url } });
    await refresh();
  };
  const save = async () => {
    if (!editing?.name?.trim()) return;
    if (!editing.api_key?.trim() || !editing.model?.trim() || !editing.base_url?.trim()) {
      setTestMessage("璇峰～鍐?API Key銆佹ā鍨嬪拰 Base URL");
      return;
    }
    setSaving(true);
    try {
      const saved = await window.storybound.saveLlmProfile(editing as Partial<LlmProfile> & { name: string; provider: string; protocol: string });
      if (saved.is_default) {
        setDraft({ ...draft, llm: { ...draft.llm, provider: saved.provider, protocol: saved.protocol, base_url: saved.base_url, api_key: saved.api_key, model: saved.model, proxy_url: saved.proxy_url } });
      }
      setEditing(null);
      await refresh();
    } finally { setSaving(false); }
  };
  const remove = async () => {
    if (!editing?.id) return;
    const next = await window.storybound.deleteLlmProfile(editing.id);
    if (next) setDraft({ ...draft, llm: { ...draft.llm, provider: next.provider, protocol: next.protocol, base_url: next.base_url, api_key: next.api_key, model: next.model, proxy_url: next.proxy_url } });
    setEditing(null);
    await refresh();
  };

  if (editing) {
    return <div className="llm-editor-page">
      <div className="llm-editor-top">
        <button onClick={() => { setEditing(null); setTestMessage(""); }}>鈥?杩斿洖</button>
        {editing.id && <button className="delete-profile" onClick={remove}><Trash2 size={15} />鍒犻櫎姝ら厤缃?/button>}
      </div>
      <div className="llm-editor-card">
        <label className="llm-field"><span>閰嶇疆鍚嶇О<small>缁欒繖涓厤缃捣涓ソ璁扮殑鍚嶅瓧</small></span><input autoFocus value={editing.name || ""} onChange={e => setEditing({ ...editing, name: e.target.value })} placeholder="渚嬪锛欳laude 涓浆閰嶇疆" /></label>
        <div className="llm-field"><span>鏈嶅姟鍟?/span><div className="provider-grid single-provider">
          <button className="selected" disabled><strong>鑷畾涔?/ 鍏朵粬</strong><small>OpenAI / Claude</small></button>
        </div>
          <p className="field-help">鍏煎 OpenAI 鎴?Claude 鍗忚鐨勭洿杩?/ 涓浆 API锛堝惈 OpenAI 瀹樻柟銆丄nthropic 瀹樻柟鍙婂悇绉嶄腑杞珯锛?/p>
        </div>
        <div className="llm-field"><span>鍗忚<small>蹇呴€?/small></span><div className="protocol-grid">
          <button className={editing.protocol === "openai" ? "selected" : ""} onClick={() => setEditing({ ...editing, protocol: "openai" })}><strong>OpenAI 鍏煎</strong><small>/chat/completions</small></button>
          <button className={editing.protocol === "anthropic" ? "selected" : ""} onClick={() => setEditing({ ...editing, protocol: "anthropic" })}><strong>Claude 鍘熺敓</strong><small>/messages</small></button>
        </div><p className="field-help">蹇呴』鎸夋湇鍔″晢鏂囨。閫夋嫨锛氭帴鍙ｅ湴鍧€鍖呭惈 /chat/completions 鏃堕€?OpenAI 鍏煎锛涙槑纭彁渚?/v1/messages 鏃舵墠閫?Claude 鍘熺敓銆傚緢澶?Claude 涓浆绔欏疄闄呬粛浣跨敤 OpenAI 鍏煎鍗忚銆?/p></div>
        <label className="llm-field"><span>API Key<small>蹇呭～</small></span><div className="secret-input">
          <input type={showKey ? "text" : "password"} value={editing.api_key || ""} onChange={e => setEditing({ ...editing, api_key: e.target.value })} />
          <button title="鏄剧ず鎴栭殣钘? onClick={() => setShowKey(value => !value)}><Eye size={16} /></button>
          <button title="澶嶅埗" onClick={() => window.storybound.writeClipboard(editing.api_key || "")}><Copy size={16} /></button>
        </div><p className="field-help">浠呯敤浜庢湰鍦拌皟鐢紝淇濆瓨鍦ㄦ湰鏈洪厤缃腑</p></label>
        <label className="llm-field"><span>妯″瀷<small>蹇呭～</small></span><input value={editing.model || ""} onChange={e => setEditing({ ...editing, model: e.target.value })} placeholder="渚嬪 claude-opus-4-6" /></label>
        <label className="llm-field"><span>Base URL<small>蹇呭～</small></span><input value={editing.base_url || ""} onChange={e => setEditing({ ...editing, base_url: e.target.value })} placeholder="https://api.example.com/v1" /><p className="field-help">鑷畾涔?API 绔偣锛涚暀绌轰娇鐢ㄦ湇鍔″晢榛樿鍦板潃</p></label>
        <label className="llm-field"><span>浠ｇ悊 URL<small>鍙€?/small></span><input value={editing.proxy_url || ""} onChange={e => setEditing({ ...editing, proxy_url: e.target.value })} placeholder="http://127.0.0.1:7890" /><p className="field-help">浠呮閰嶇疆鐢熸晥锛岀粯鍥惧拰 TTS 涓嶅彈褰卞搷銆傜暀绌哄垯鐩磋繛銆?/p></label>
        <div className="llm-editor-actions">
          <button className="secondary" onClick={async () => {
            const candidate = { ...draft, llm: { ...draft.llm, provider: editing.provider || "custom", protocol: editing.protocol || "openai", api_key: editing.api_key || "", base_url: editing.base_url || "", model: editing.model || "", proxy_url: editing.proxy_url || "" } };
            const result = await window.storybound.testConfig("llm", candidate);
            setTestMessage(result.message);
          }}><Sparkles size={15} />娴嬭瘯杩炴帴</button>
          <button className="primary" disabled={saving} onClick={save}><Save size={15} />{saving ? "淇濆瓨涓€? : "淇濆瓨閰嶇疆"}</button>
        </div>
        {testMessage && <div className="connection-result">{testMessage}</div>}
      </div>
    </div>;
  }

  return <>
    <div className="llm-list-heading">
      <PanelHeading icon={Sparkles} title="LLM 閰嶇疆" desc="淇濆瓨澶氫釜閰嶇疆锛屽垱寤轰换鍔℃椂涓€閿垏鎹? />
      <button className="secondary" onClick={startCreate}><Plus size={16} />鏂板缓閰嶇疆</button>
    </div>
    <div className="profile-count">宸蹭繚瀛?<strong>{profiles.length}</strong> 涓厤缃?/div>
    <div className="llm-profile-list">{profiles.map(profile => <article className={profile.is_default ? "active" : ""} key={profile.id}>
      <button className="profile-radio" onClick={() => activate(profile)} aria-label={`鍚敤 ${profile.name}`}><i /></button>
      <div className="profile-main">
        <h3>{profile.name}{profile.is_default ? <span>浣跨敤涓?/span> : null}</h3>
        <p>{providerName(profile.provider)} 路 {profile.model || "鏈～鍐欐ā鍨?} 路 {maskKey(profile.api_key)}</p>
      </div>
      <button className="edit-profile" onClick={() => { setEditing({ ...profile, provider: "custom", protocol: profile.protocol === "anthropic" ? "anthropic" : "openai" }); setTestMessage(""); setShowKey(false); }}><Settings size={15} />缂栬緫</button>
      <button className="icon-btn" onClick={() => setEditing({ ...profile, provider: "custom", protocol: profile.protocol === "anthropic" ? "anthropic" : "openai" })}><MoreHorizontal size={17} /></button>
    </article>)}</div>
    <div className="llm-tip">鍒涘缓浠诲姟鏃跺彲鍦ㄣ€屼娇鐢ㄦā鍨嬨€嶄笅鎷夐噷绉掑垏 路 涓嶅悓閰嶇疆鍙互浣跨敤涓嶅悓浠ｇ悊銆丅ase URL 鍜?API Key</div>
  </>;
}

const providerName = (provider: string) => ({
  custom: "鑷畾涔?
}[provider] || provider);
const imageProviderName = (provider: string) => {
  const value = String(provider || "");
  if (value.startsWith("custom_image")) return "foxcode";
  if (value.toLowerCase().startsWith("apimart")) return "Apimart";
  return value;
};
const maskKey = (key: string) => key ? `${key.slice(0, 4)}鈥⑩€⑩€⑩€?{key.slice(-4)}` : "鏈～鍐?Key";

function ImageSettings({ draft, setDraft }: { draft: AppConfig; setDraft: (next: AppConfig) => void }) {
  const provider = draft.image_provider;
  const [testMessage, setTestMessage] = useState("");
  const [showAdvanced, setShowAdvanced] = useState(false);
  const [showKey, setShowKey] = useState(false);
  const [testing, setTesting] = useState(false);
  const ratios = ["9:16", "3:4", "1:1", "4:3", "16:9"];
  const setProvider = (next: string) => {
    setTestMessage("");
    setDraft({ ...draft, image_provider: next });
  };
  const updateCustom = (patch: Partial<AppConfig["custom_image"]>) => setDraft({ ...draft, custom_image: { ...draft.custom_image, ...patch } });
  const updateApimart = (patch: Partial<AppConfig["apimart"]>) => setDraft({ ...draft, apimart: { ...draft.apimart, ...patch } });
  const updateModelscope = (patch: Partial<AppConfig["modelscope"]>) => setDraft({ ...draft, modelscope: { ...draft.modelscope, ...patch } });
  const updateRunningHub = (patch: Partial<AppConfig["runninghub"]>) => setDraft({ ...draft, runninghub: { ...draft.runninghub, ...patch } });
  const common = provider === "modelscope" ? draft.modelscope : provider === "runninghub" ? draft.runninghub : provider === "apimart" ? draft.apimart : draft.custom_image;
  const testLabel = ["custom_image", "apimart"].includes(provider) ? "娴嬭瘯鐢熷浘锛堜細娑堣€楅搴︼級" : "娴嬭瘯杩炴帴";

  return <>
    <PanelHeading icon={Image} title="AI 缁樺浘" desc="鍒嗛暅鍥剧墖鐢熸垚 路 鍙湪 foxcode 涓?Apimart 涔嬮棿鍒囨崲" />
    <div className="image-provider-tabs">
      <button className={provider === "custom_image" ? "active" : ""} onClick={() => setProvider("custom_image")}>foxcode{provider === "custom_image" && <small>浣跨敤涓?/small>}</button>
      <button className={provider === "apimart" ? "active" : ""} onClick={() => setProvider("apimart")}>Apimart{provider === "apimart" && <small>浣跨敤涓?/small>}</button>

        </div>

            <label>浠诲姟 ID 瀛楁<input value={draft.custom_image.task_id_field} onChange={e => updateCustom({ task_id_field: e.target.value })} /></label>
